import re

with open('src/cpu/pred/sr.cc', 'r') as f:
    content = f.read()

new_hash = """
uint32_t hash_ut(uint64_t pc, int table_idx) {
    uint64_t h = pc ^ (pc >> 2);
    h = h * 11400714819323198485ull;
    h = h ^ (h >> 32);
    h = h * 11400714819323198485ull;
    h = h ^ (h >> 32);
    return (uint32_t)(h) + table_idx * 0x9e3779b9;
}

uint32_t hash_wt(uint64_t pc, uint32_t digest, int table_idx) {
    uint64_t h = pc ^ (pc >> 2);
    h = h ^ ((uint64_t)digest << 11);
    h = h * 11400714819323198485ull;
    h = h ^ (h >> 32);
    h = h * 11400714819323198485ull;
    h = h ^ (h >> 32);
    return (uint32_t)(h) + table_idx * 0x9e3779b9;
}
"""

content = re.sub(r'uint32_t hash_ut.*?\}', '', content, flags=re.DOTALL)
content = re.sub(r'uint32_t hash_wt.*?\}', '', content, flags=re.DOTALL)
content = content.replace('// These hashes aren\'t specified exactly by the paper, so we\'ll use simple ones\n\n\n', '// These hashes aren\'t specified exactly by the paper, so we\'ll use simple ones\n' + new_hash)

with open('src/cpu/pred/sr.cc', 'w') as f:
    f.write(content)
EOF

   252	void
   253	TAGE_SC_L_TAGE_64KB::handleAllocAndUReset(bool alloc, bool taken,
   254	                                          TAGEBase::BranchInfo *bi, int nrand)
   255	{
   256	    if (! alloc) {
   257	        return;
   258	    }
   259	
   260	    int penalty = 0;
   261	    int numAllocated = 0;
   262	    bool maxAllocReached = false;
   263	
   264	    for (int I = calcDep(bi); I < nHistoryTables; I += 2) {
   265	        // Handle the 2-way associativity for allocation
   266	        for (int j = 0; j < 2; ++j) {
   267	            int i = ((j == 0) ? I : (I ^ 1)) + 1;
   268	            if (noSkip[i]) {
   269	                if (gtable[i][bi->tableIndices[i]].u == 0) {
   270	                    int8_t ctr = gtable[i][bi->tableIndices[i]].ctr;
   271	                    if (abs (2 * ctr + 1) <= 3) {
   272	                        gtable[i][bi->tableIndices[i]].tag = bi->tableTags[i];
   273	                        gtable[i][bi->tableIndices[i]].ctr = taken ? 0 : -1;
   274	                        numAllocated++;
   275	                        maxAllocReached = (numAllocated == maxNumAlloc);
   276	                        I += 2;
   277	                        break;
   278	                    } else {
   279	                        if (gtable[i][bi->tableIndices[i]].ctr > 0) {
   280	                            gtable[i][bi->tableIndices[i]].ctr--;
   281	                        } else {
   282	                            gtable[i][bi->tableIndices[i]].ctr++;
   283	                        }
   284	                    }
   285	                } else {
   286	                    penalty++;
   287	                }
   288	            }
   289	        }
   290	        if (maxAllocReached) {
   291	            break;
   292	        }
   293	    }
   294	
   295	    tCounter += (penalty - 2 * numAllocated);
   296	
   297	    handleUReset();
   298	}

   407	TAGE_SC_L_64KB_StatisticalCorrector::onRename(const gem5::o3::DynInstPtr &inst)
   408	{
   409	    // decode_cycle_or_count_tick
   410	    for (int r = 0; r < SR_NUM_LOGICAL_REGS; ++r) {
   411	        if (sR_RegTable[r].decay_ctr > 0) {
   412	            sR_RegTable[r].decay_ctr--;

   414	            sR_RegTable[r].valid = false;
   415	        }
   416	    }
   417	
   418	    // allocate_register
   419	    for (int i = 0; i < inst->numDestRegs(); i++) {
   420	        const gem5::RegId &arch = inst->destRegIdx(i);
   421	        if (!arch.isRenameable()) continue;
   422	
   423	        int spec_reg = -1;

   425	            if (arch.index() < 31) spec_reg = arch.index(); // X0-X30
   426	            else if (arch.index() == gem5::ArmISA::int_reg::_SpxIdx) spec_reg = 31; // SP
   427	        } else if (arch.is(gem5::VecRegClass)) {
   428	            if (arch.index() < 32) spec_reg = 32 + arch.index(); // V0-V31
   429	        } else if (arch.is(gem5::CCRegClass)) {
   430	            if (arch.index() <= 2) spec_reg = 64; // Nz, C, V
   431	        }
   432	
   433	        if (spec_reg != -1 && spec_reg < SR_NUM_LOGICAL_REGS) {
   434	            sR_RegTable[spec_reg].valid = false;
   435	            // The payload takes the ROB index
   436	            sR_RegTable[spec_reg].payload = inst->seqNum & ((1 << SR_ROB_IDX_BITS) - 1);
   437	        }
   438	    }
   439	}

   289	
   290	uint16_t
   291	TAGE_SC_L_TAGE_64KB::gtag(ThreadID tid, Addr pc, int bank) const
   292	{
   293	    // very similar to the TAGE implementation, but w/o shifting the pc
   294	    Addr shifted_pc = pc >> instShiftAmt;
   295	    int tag = shifted_pc ^ threadHistory[tid].computeTags[0][bank].comp ^
   296	              (threadHistory[tid].computeTags[1][bank].comp << 1);
   297	
   298	    return (tag & ((1ULL << tagTableTagWidths[bank]) - 1));
   299	}

   128	         & ((1 << logBias) -1);
   129	}

   412	void
   413	TAGE_SC_L_64KB_StatisticalCorrector::onRename(const gem5::o3::DynInstPtr &inst)
   414	{
   415	    // decode_cycle_or_count_tick
   416	    for (int r = 0; r < SR_NUM_LOGICAL_REGS; ++r) {
   417	        if (sR_RegTable[r].decay_ctr > 0) {
   418	            sR_RegTable[r].decay_ctr--;
   419	        } else {
   420	            sR_RegTable[r].valid = false;
   421	        }
   422	    }
   423	
   424	    // allocate_register
   425	    for (int i = 0; i < inst->numDestRegs(); i++) {
   426	        const gem5::RegId &arch = inst->destRegIdx(i);
   427	        if (!arch.isRenameable()) continue;
   428	
   429	        int spec_reg = -1;
   430	        if (arch.is(gem5::IntRegClass)) {
   431	            if (arch.index() < 31) spec_reg = arch.index(); // X0-X30
   432	            else if (arch.index() == gem5::ArmISA::int_reg::_SpxIdx) spec_reg = 31; // SP
   433	        } else if (arch.is(gem5::VecRegClass)) {
   434	            if (arch.index() < 32) spec_reg = 32 + arch.index(); // V0-V31
   435	        } else if (arch.is(gem5::CCRegClass)) {
   436	            if (arch.index() <= 2) spec_reg = 64; // Nz, C, V
   437	        }
   438	
   439	        if (spec_reg != -1 && spec_reg < SR_NUM_LOGICAL_REGS) {
   440	            sR_RegTable[spec_reg].valid = false;
   441	            // The payload takes the ROB index
   442	            sR_RegTable[spec_reg].payload = inst->seqNum & ((1 << SR_ROB_IDX_BITS) - 1);
   443	        }
   444	    }
   445	}

   412	void
   413	TAGE_SC_L_64KB_StatisticalCorrector::onRename(const gem5::o3::DynInstPtr &inst)
   414	{
   415	    // decode_cycle_or_count_tick
   416	    for (int r = 0; r < SR_NUM_LOGICAL_REGS; ++r) {
   417	        if (sR_RegTable[r].decay_ctr > 0) {
   418	            sR_RegTable[r].decay_ctr--;
   419	        } else {
   420	            sR_RegTable[r].valid = false;
   421	        }
   422	    }
   423	
   424	    // allocate_register
   425	    for (int i = 0; i < inst->numDestRegs(); i++) {
   426	        const gem5::RegId &arch = inst->destRegIdx(i);
   427	        if (!arch.isRenameable()) continue;
   428	
   429	        int spec_reg = -1;
   430	        if (arch.is(gem5::IntRegClass)) {
   431	            if (arch.index() < 31) spec_reg = arch.index(); // X0-X30
   432	            else if (arch.index() == gem5::ArmISA::int_reg::_SpxIdx) spec_reg = 31; // SP
   433	        } else if (arch.is(gem5::VecRegClass)) {
   434	            if (arch.index() < 32) spec_reg = 32 + arch.index(); // V0-V31
   435	        } else if (arch.is(gem5::CCRegClass)) {
   436	            if (arch.index() <= 2) spec_reg = 64; // Nz, C, V
   437	        }
   438	
   439	        if (spec_reg != -1 && spec_reg < SR_NUM_LOGICAL_REGS) {
   440	            sR_RegTable[spec_reg].valid = false;
   441	            // The payload takes the ROB index
   442	            sR_RegTable[spec_reg].payload = inst->seqNum & ((1 << SR_ROB_IDX_BITS) - 1);
   443	        }
   444	    }
   445	}

I have fixed the issue! The extra brace that was ending the `gPredictions` scope too early was hiding at line 212. It was causing `lsum` to be out of scope for the subsequent code and pushing the remaining functions into the wrong scope, leading to undefined reference linker errors.

I noticed that some functions (`gIndexLogsSubstr`, `scHistoryUpdate`, `scRecordHistState`, and `scRestoreHistState`) were also missing from the file `src/cpu/pred/tage_sc_l_64KB.cc`, which was causing the linker error `undefined reference` in the build process. I went back and extracted them from `git show HEAD:src/cpu/pred/tage_sc_l_64KB.cc` and inserted them back where they belong right after `gPredictions`.

The build has succeeded in the container without errors. I tried running the gate script but could not locate `gate.sh` or `gate.py` in the container's checkout. The codebase is now successfully compiled with all the logic fixes in place and should be ready for the gate execution.
